Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The cycle variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 877 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 447 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by cycle generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 447. QED.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the cycle decomposition. By minimality, the element a^288 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 821-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
